# 527: You Guessed It Again

Night fell.

Yet Opelucid City remained far from quiet. Its vibrant neon lights twinkled beneath the bright moon above.

Johan and his companions arrived in the outskirts of the city.

"Are you sure something's going to happen?" Caitlin asked with a trace of skepticism.

From the outside, Opelucid City looked just as peaceful as ever.

"I'm not sure either," Johan shrugged honestly.

It was the truth.

Even though he knew Team Plasma would likely make a move on Opelucid City—especially concerning Kyurem—in the original storyline, Team Plasma's actions here were already under the leadership of Colress. And at that time, Colress had fully mastered the power of Kyurem.

So sure, he could guess that Team Plasma might target Kyurem…

But how they would capture it, when, and how long it would take them to weaponize its power—those were complete unknowns.

He couldn't exactly go to the Unova League and say, "Hey, I have a feeling they're going to make a move on Kyurem, so you should just keep guarding the Giant Chasm forever."

It was just like in Hoenn—he knew Team Magma and Team Aqua would awaken Groudon and Kyogre, but when exactly they'd act? Who knew?

All he could do was make preparations—like befriending Rayquaza.

Same here.

He could only guess that Team Plasma might be using Mistralton City as a decoy while making a move on Opelucid—but he had no way of being 100% sure.

And let's be real—just one guy's hunch wasn't enough to redirect the entire League's focus.

Team Plasma was actually attacking Mistralton.

You think the League would just ignore that on a maybe?

And yeah, maybe the League also suspected a misdirection.

But it was an open stratagem—hard to avoid.

A full-on assault on a major city? The League had no choice but to respond.

Even if Johan explained how critical the DNA Splicers were, what could they do?

Drayden—the current holder of the DNA Splicers—was already one of the strongest Trainers in Unova. Probably just below the Elite Four.

If even Drayden couldn't hold out, how many reinforcements would it take?

And Alder couldn't just sit in Opelucid all day.

There was another thing too:

Johan wasn't even sure Colress could wield Kyurem's power this quickly.

Without Kyurem, unless Team Plasma sent half the Seven Sages, there was no way they'd beat Drayden.

The more pressing problem was—

This world felt "chaotic" to Johan.

Elements from the anime, movies, manga, and games all seemed mixed in. Timelines weren't matching up.

The assault on Opelucid? That was from Pokémon Generations, not even the "main canon."

So, in the end, this was all just Johan's guess.

If nothing happened, great. The three of them wouldn't have made much difference in Mistralton anyway.

But if something did go down in Opelucid, they might be able to help.

Still, Johan wasn't totally reckless.

He had sent Alder a short warning with his suspicions.

And Alder trusted him enough to quietly place a few reinforcements nearby.

At this point, Johan had accepted that he might be a walking butterfly effect.

Wherever he went, something happened.

He wouldn't be wrong to call himself the Pokémon World's own "Grim Reaper."

"You mean you don't know?!" Caitlin's eyes widened.

Johan snapped, "What do you expect? I'm not psychic—or a Team Plasma mole. Not knowing is pretty normal!"

If predicting the moves of evil teams were so easy, he'd have long since dismantled Team Magma, Aqua, and Galactic, earned his honors, settled down, and married a rich girl by now.

"So what do we do now?" Cynthia asked softly as she patted Caitlin's head.

Caitlin immediately calmed down—going from angry kitten to tame lapcat with just a stroke.

"Let's go find Drayden," Johan said.

He still hoped nothing would happen.

But at this point, even he was scared to jinx things.

"Sounds good," Cynthia nodded.

Caitlin wrinkled her cute little nose and took out her phone. "I'll let the others know to meet us there."

"The others" were their backup—some of Caitlin's own companions.

Team Plasma's flying fortress hadn't yet appeared, but their people had already arrived.

"Sage Zinzolin!"

Standing on a rooftop in his bulky winter garb, Zinzolin's presence made Drayden's expression harden.

One of the Seven Sages had come to Opelucid?

Johan's warning echoed in his mind—about Team Plasma targeting the DNA Splicers.

And now, only a few hours later, here they were.

"Drayden," Zinzolin said with a sly smile.

The scrawny Sage looked almost comical in his oversized robes and hat as he gazed down at the muscular Dragon Master.

"Please lend Team Plasma the DNA Splicers. We promise to return it after we're done."

"Hmph!!"

Drayden snorted.

So it really was about the Splicers.

He didn't waste words.

Zinzolin was nothing but a coward hiding behind a fake title.

Drayden tossed out his Poké Ball.

"RAWWWR!!"

Haxorus burst forth with a mighty roar.

If you want it, come get it!

Drayden crossed his arms and stood tall—pride of a Dragon Master radiating from him.

"Tch."

Zinzolin wasn't surprised.

"Let's see if you're as strong as they say, Dragon Master."

He waved a hand.

"Weaviles!!"

Two Weavile leapt from the shadows behind him and launched a sneak attack on Drayden and Haxorus.

Zinzolin specialized in Ice-types—one of the reasons he was so obsessed with Kyurem.

He had no fear of Drayden.

Ice beats Dragon. Advantage: him.

"Weavile! Ice Beam!!"

The twin Weaviles coldly aimed and fired twin silver beams from their claws mid-air.

Drayden didn't even flinch. He crossed his arms and looked unimpressed.

Sure, Ice beats Dragon.

But that depends on which Ice-types you're using!

These Weavile?

Not good enough.

"RAWRRR!!"

Haxorus roared, charging into the beams instead of retreating.

Its body erupted with crimson dragonfire that morphed mid-air into a dragon-shaped flare, colliding head-on with the beams.

BOOM!!

Smoke and frost burst outward.

But before the fog could clear, Haxorus tore through it, closing the distance instantly.

Crimson axes gleamed coldly.

BAM! BAM!

Two Dragon Claws sent the Weaviles flying.

Zinzolin narrowed his eyes.

So the rumors about Drayden were true.

"But no matter how strong you are—you're just one man."

He turned and whispered:

"Commence."

BOOM!!!

An explosion rocked a nearby building, causing panic across the city.

This was what made evil teams such a pain.

They always used civilians as leverage—forcing the League to hesitate.

Drayden's face darkened.

Zinzolin, meanwhile, smiled.

Because he wasn't alone.

While he hadn't brought many to avoid suspicion, those who came were Team Plasma's elite.

And more importantly—

Ghetsis had allowed him to bring the Dark Triad.

"Continue until he hands it over," Zinzolin ordered.

But then—

"This is Officer Jenny from Opelucid! You're under arrest!"

"International Police! Move and you'll regret it!"

Huh?

Zinzolin blinked.

What?!

The League was supposed to be distracted by Mistralton. This was an open tactic. Why were cops and Interpol in Opelucid?

"Hmph!" Drayden sneered. "Nothing but a band of cowards."

Zinzolin's smile faltered.

But only for a moment.

"Dark Triad! Capture Drayden!"

Buzz—

Three shadows emerged from the darkness, surrounding Drayden on all sides.

If they could take him down, everything else was easy.

But just then—

"Dragonite, Dragon Rush!!"

"Garchomp, Dragon Rush!!"

"Gothitelle—wait, no, use Psychic!!"

Caitlin's inner rage: Why are those two always so in sync?!

From the heavens, two dragons wrapped in burning flame streaked across the sky like falling stars, hurtling down in a blaze of red light.

For a moment—

The people of Opelucid thought they saw a meteor… or perhaps, the legendary twin dragons of their city's mythology.

The burning light tore through the night sky like a blade.

Zinzolin and the Dark Triad squinted against the glare.

But to Drayden?

This was what Dragon-type Pokémon were all about!

From atop a high-rise—

Johan stood, coat fluttering in the wind, one hand adjusting his hat, the other in his pocket, eyes cold behind his monocle.

Beside him, Caitlin pouted—still annoyed she misspoke earlier.

Cynthia tucked her golden hair behind her ear, smiling softly:

"Looks like you guessed right again."

Johan's mouth twitched.

Being right wasn't always a good thing…

And the way she said again—could she be more smug?

Still, something was off.

If it was just Zinzolin and the Dark Triad, Team Plasma was still underwhelming.

Then—

BOOOOM!!!

A deafening explosion rocked the city.

The battle for Opelucid had begun.

# 528: Freeze Opelucid City? Ask Us First!

"Is that... Teacher Johan with Cynthia and Caitlin?"

Some distance from the center of Opelucid City, two Trainers suddenly came to a stop.

One of them, holding a book and wearing a black skirt, with round-frame glasses glinting under the light, seemed quite familiar with Teacher Johan.

This was Wanlong, a renowned young writer from the Unova region, and also a Trainer with formidable strength.

"It looks like it's already started."

Beside Wanlong was a tall, muscular young man with a buzz cut and a hardened face, dressed in martial arts gear.

This was Lenwu, Unova Champion Alder's disciple—a martial artist specializing in Fighting-type Pokémon, and also quite powerful.

Though neither had yet become members of the Unova Elite Four, their strength and talent were undeniable.

Wanlong focused on writing as her primary career, while Pokémon training remained her side pursuit. After all, in the world of Pokémon, not having a main and side job was rare.

Lenwu, on the other hand, was devoted to combat and strength—perhaps even to an obsessive degree—and was still under observation.

Both of them, however, could play a crucial role in this unfolding crisis.

Wanlong and Lenwu exchanged glances, each spotting the flicker of excitement in the other's eyes.

Wanlong had long heard from Diantha and Cynthia that Teacher Johan had exceptional writing talent. The wildly popular Meowth & Pichu was written by him. Other screenplays like Infernape Wreaks Havoc on Mt. Coronet, Dreamspace, and Gardevoir and the Seven Taggers were also his work.

Some scripts hadn't even been released due to technical reasons.

Wanlong was eager to discuss writing with him.

As for Lenwu? His reason was simple.

As a martial artist, Johan's reputation was stellar.

Praised highly by both Master Mustard, the strongest former Champion of Galar, and Bruno, the Kanto Elite Four member—how could he not want to challenge him?

Even his master Alder had nothing but praise for Johan.

Lenwu respected Alder deeply but didn't always listen to him. Still, the gap in their strength always frustrated him.

Now, meeting someone whom even his master admired—a fellow renowned "martial artist"—Lenwu felt his fists itch with anticipation.

But first, they had to help resolve the situation in Opelucid.

Inside the Team Plasma airship, Colress sat calmly in the control room, eyes fixed on the projection on his tablet.

That Zinzolin would fail to take Drayden? Expected.

Even with the Dark Triad? Still expected.

Unova's forces might be drawn to Mistralton, but that didn't mean they were helpless elsewhere.

In Colress's eyes, Zinzolin and the Dark Triad were just scouts—to test Opelucid's defenses.

And they were doing a fine job.

But there was something unexpected.

Colress smiled faintly at the figure on-screen.

"Teacher Johan… we meet again."

Had Johan unraveled Team Plasma's plan? Or just happened to be there?

Colress suspected the latter—but it didn't matter.

"In the face of absolute power, guesswork doesn't matter."

He leaned back and pressed a button.

Buzz—

A peculiar pulse spread outward from the Team Plasma airship.

At the same time, the hidden ship, nestled in clouds above Opelucid, powered up and advanced—soon appearing within Johan and his companions' line of sight.

The sound of turbines and engines echoed.

"Look!" Caitlin raised her hand and looked up.

From among the skyscrapers, the head of the enormous flying battleship emerged. Following was its massive hull, wings, rotors, and thrusters.

The enormous vessel loomed like a fortress in the sky, casting a deepening shadow over Opelucid—a darkness ready to devour everything.

Johan and Cynthia's expressions turned grim.

Team Plasma's airship.

"So it really came…" Cynthia murmured.

They stood on a mid-height rooftop, wind tugging at their clothes as the monstrous craft induced growing panic among the citizens below.

More than that—

The strange pulses emanating from the airship were riling up Pokémon throughout the city!

"What the—?"

"Colress's Machine." Johan narrowed his eyes.

This was meant to spread chaos on a large scale?

And of course, it wasn't just that.

Colress's smile deepened.

He whispered to himself, staring at Johan on-screen:

"Of course not…"

Madness flared in his eyes.

He turned to the rear of the ship, where a massive container sat, filled with strange liquid. Inside slumbered a grotesque, dragon-like beast.

Kyurem.

"More dosage!"

The liquid bubbled.

Kyurem's closed eyes twisted with pain. Ice energy surged from its body—rapidly sucked up by the surrounding machinery, freezing the entire chamber in seconds.

Exactly what Colress wanted.

Control Kyurem? Not yet possible.

But control its energy? That was enough!

Colress stood with arms wide open, glasses gleaming with madness.

Let the world witness the genius of Colress!

He returned to calm. "Prepare to fire."

"Yes, sir!"

Three… Two… One… FIRE!

BOOOOM!!!

Colress smiled faintly. "Let's see how you handle this, Johan."

Back in Opelucid—

"They're coming!" Johan gritted his teeth.

Colress had played it carefully—using Zinzolin and the Triad to draw attention, then unleashing chaos from above.

And now—

Mewtwo appeared beside Johan, eyes narrowing as it looked skyward.

"What?" Cynthia and Caitlin didn't yet understand.

But then they saw it: a metal cannon extended, glowing with chilling blue energy.

Their eyes widened.

Wanlong and Lenwu, far off, were similarly stunned.

A massive icy beam roared down from the sky, striking the city's tallest tower.

CRACK!

In an instant, the tower froze solid.

And it didn't stop.

Ice energy spread outward like a tsunami, freezing all in its path.

Freeze Opelucid City.

Drayden's heart trembled.

Zinzolin, meanwhile, sneered triumphantly.

"Don't want to hand over the Splicers? Then let us show you our power."

He spread his arms wide.

"Welcome the frozen world!"

Wanlong and Lenwu stared in horror.

What could they do?

This wasn't something a normal Trainer or Pokémon could deal with.

Panic seized the city.

People screamed.

And just then—

WHOOSH—

A single figure flew straight into the beam of ice!

Mewtwo.

Serious-faced, wrapped in psychic energy, it raised one arm high.

Ice and psychic power collided—shards flying everywhere.

And somehow, the beam… didn't advance further.

"Cynthia! Caitlin!"

Johan mounted Dragonite and soared toward the nearest rooftop to the ship.

"Freeze Opelucid? Ask us first!"

Everyone watching fell silent.

Who was this Pokémon that could stop such an apocalyptic blast?

Wanlong and Lenwu exchanged stunned glances.

"Could that be… Johan's?"

A legendary?

Of course.

"Still think he's not the 'Legendary Guy'?" Lenwu muttered.

"Impossible!!" Zinzolin roared.

That was Kyurem's power!

How could a Pokémon block that?!

Drayden breathed a sigh of relief.

No civilians near the tower—thank goodness.

And with the beam blocked, the worst had been avoided. For now.

People began to stop fleeing.

Their hope reignited.

"It's Teacher Johan!"

"And Miss Cynthia and Lady Caitlin!"

Even Colress was stunned.

That Pokémon—he'd never seen or even heard of it before.

"A new variable…"

He didn't panic.

He smiled.

"Excellent."

"Increase the dose—fire again!"

"But sir…" the Plasma grunt hesitated.

More dosage? Kyurem might wake up!

But Colress's chilling gaze silenced him.

"Yes, sir!"

ROOOOARR!!!

Kyurem's pain echoed—but more power surged.

Colress gave one final order:

"Send my little gift… to Teacher Johan."

"Yes!"

The ship's bay opened—releasing hordes of Klink and Klang.

Colress's favorite: machine-like loyalty.

Inside the beam, Mewtwo winced, hands raised, still holding off the assault—but visibly struggling now.

On the rooftop, Johan watched.

He took a deep breath.

The cold stung his lungs.

His Pokémon appeared beside him in flashes of red.

"Ninetales!"

"Oowoo~!!"

The icy energy was like fuel for her.

As for the gears? Johan didn't even glance.

Cynthia and Caitlin would handle them.

He looked through the ship's cockpit window—and locked eyes with Colress.

Colress smiled provocatively.

"So what now, Teacher Johan?"

Johan adjusted his glasses, hand brushing the Key Stone at his waist.

What now?

What else?

Colress used a whole city as a bargaining chip.

It was time to respond in kind.

His hand tightened on the Key Stone.

His eyes met Mewtwo's.

Mewtwo nodded.

Let's try.

Johan took another deep breath.

Buzz—

"Mewtwo, Mega Evolve!!"

# 529: Super Mewtwo Y!

"Mewtwo, Mega Evolve!!"

Johan shouted.

Buzz—

The Key Stone on his hand flared with intense heat, as if an invisible power bridged Johan and Mewtwo.

At the same time—

Mewtwo felt the shackles on its Mega Stone suddenly snap. The power of Mega Evolution surged forth like silk from a silkworm, enveloping its body.

The light of evolution washed over it, bringing with it a sensation unlike anything it had experienced before.

So this is what evolution feels like?

This is what it means to have hearts and minds connected?

This... is the power of a bond?

In an instant, Mewtwo seemed to understand so much—gaining a clarity it had never had before.

Already powerful in psychic strength, Mewtwo now felt its abilities soar to new heights, and its control over them became almost instinctual.

Caitlin opened her mouth in shock.

Hadn't Johan said that Mewtwo wasn't his Pokémon?

How then...

And more incredibly—

Mewtwo could Mega Evolve?!

That's not scientific!

Not far away, Wanlong and Lenwu were equally stunned.

A legendary Pokémon can Mega Evolve?

But more than that...

"That's definitely Johan's Pokémon," Wanlong murmured.

Boom!!

The Mega energy retracted, and Mewtwo reappeared in a brand-new form before everyone's eyes.

No—more accurately, this was Mega Mewtwo Y!

Smaller in size than regular Mewtwo, with more compact limbs and body, its head now sported headphone-like protrusions. A pink-purple tendril—part hair, part tail—stretched from its head to its feet.

Mega Mewtwo Y!

A Psychic-type Pokémon whose Special Attack had reached absolute extremes!

Stat-wise, Mega Mewtwo Y boasted a terrifying 780 base total, with a Special Attack stat of 194—the highest among all Pokémon!

"It actually worked..."

Honestly—

Even though Johan had been somewhat confident, seeing Mewtwo Mega Evolve with his aid still surprised him.

But more than surprise...

Was pressure.

Tremendous pressure.

This wasn't like maintaining Mega Gengar.

Johan guessed it was partly because Mewtwo was far stronger, and partly because their bond wasn't as deep yet as his bond with Gengar.

Plus, this was Mewtwo's first Mega.

"Mewtwo, make it quick!"

Johan's voice echoed in Mega Mewtwo Y's mind, strained and heavy.

Mewtwo, linked to him, could clearly sense Johan's condition.

And so—

Mega Mewtwo Y raised its head, and its crimson eyes surged with psychic light. An overwhelming wave of psychic power, now many times greater than before, exploded out!

RUMBLE—

Before Colress's disbelieving eyes, the resolute Mega Mewtwo Y released its power without reserve, charging straight through Kyurem's Ice Cannon toward the Team Plasma airship!

Like a sledgehammer through wood.

Truly overwhelming power, shattering the previous equilibrium in an instant.

"Holy sh—" muttered an International Police agent watching the feed.

Is this really our superior officer?

Since when was Interpol this cracked?!

Wanlong gently flipped her hair and clutched her book.

"I said it—battles are boring. Is the 'Youth Masters Championship' even worth attending?"

This Mewtwo... was unbeatable.

Even Lenwu, proud as he was, couldn't find words. If Johan sent this Pokémon to the tournament—

Pray to the gods not to face him too early.

Compared to their shock—

Cynthia, used to Johan's brand of "routine," stayed calm.

This? Just another Tuesday.

"What?!"

Colress finally lost composure.

In his mind—

Even if Mewtwo was a legendary and Kyurem was too, it should've been an even fight.

At worst? Mewtwo 60, Kyurem 40.

But Kyurem had his support—a genius scientist's double-strengthening, double-barrel ice cannon!

And yet—

His top-tier tech was obliterated like paper!

Science lost to friendship?!

"No! Impossible! Science is truth! Machines are loyal! We haven't lost!"

He roared at the Plasma members.

"Fire again! More dosage!"

The members flinched, cold sweat pouring down their backs.

Another dose?

Even if it worked... what if Kyurem fully woke up?

But before they could act—

BOOM!!

The Plasma ship trembled violently. A screen flickered on, showing external footage—

Mewtwo's psychic energy, like a drill, punched through the Ice Cannon, reaching the barrel itself.

Then—

A flash of blue light from Mega Mewtwo Y's eyes.

The entire cannon, built with Plasma's vast resources, was ripped off like a toy!

Gulp—

A Plasma member swallowed hard.

How the hell do we fight that?

"Full assault!"

Colress's icy voice snapped through the room.

...Assault?

On what, exactly?!

But Colress remained calm.

"It won't last much longer."

As a scientist devoted to pushing Pokémon to their limits, he knew plenty about Mega Evolution.

From Johan's reaction, the time Mewtwo spent in Mega form, and the collected data—

It wouldn't last.

Sure enough—

Just as he said, the energy surrounding Mega Mewtwo Y began to unravel.

On the rooftop, Johan—supported by Cynthia—gasped for breath.

To get Mewtwo to Mega Evolve, and for that long? Already a massive feat.

Back when he first Mega Evolved Gengar? Whew...

And Mewtwo, too, felt it—its first time using such power left it drained.

The Plasma crew finally exhaled.

It's over.

"We attack now!"

More attack?

But none dared disobey Colress.

"Yes, sir!"

With the order given, Colress turned and walked into the ship.

Passing through doors and alarms, past freezing air, into the deepest chamber.

Huff—

His breath turned white. Frost coated his glasses and cheeks.

Even this—just Kyurem's residual energy—was this cold?

Kyurem truly was the Strongest Dragon on Earth.

But still...

Just an uncontrollable beast.

In the massive incubation chamber, Kyurem's aura swelled. Its closed eyes... fluttered.

It was awakening!

Colress smiled faintly.

Expected.

"Did I lose? Perhaps. But—"

He pushed his glasses up.

"Just because I can't win... doesn't mean I can't force a draw."

He reached for the cold, metal override lever and yanked it down hard.

Bubble-bubble—

Kyurem's tank boiled with activity.

ROOOOAAAR!!!

A savage, guttural roar filled the ship.

Colress grinned.

After one last look at Kyurem, he turned and entered an escape pod.

Because—

Though Mega Mewtwo's power had faded, it wasn't helpless.

If he stayed...

Forget the Splicers—he'd be caught.

So—

He gave orders for Team Plasma to continue attacking, fully awaken Kyurem...

And fled.

Because he had more inventions to create. He still needed time, and resources!

"Next time, Teacher Johan... we'll have another match."

As for Kyurem—

He caught it once.

He could catch it again.

And next time—

He'd truly unleash its full power.

Psshh—

The pod launched.

Colress: I'm outta here.

With Johan and Mewtwo still weak, when else would he run?

"That bastard..."

Johan and the others spotted the escape pod—but couldn't pursue.

Plasma's forces were still blocking the way.

Worse—

The Plasma crew was now the most confused of all.

"???"

Didn't you just order us to attack?

Now you're gone?!

And that pod—it was fast, clearly modded.

BOOM!!!

Suddenly—

A massive explosion rocked the ship.

ROOOAAR!!!

A feral dragon's cry echoed.

Temperatures plummeted. Snowflakes danced.

The ice that had been halted began again—frost crawled over every window in Opelucid.

Colress had left one hell of a parting gift.

No wonder he wasn't worried about being chased.

Now—

The enormous Plasma airship began to tilt, groaning like a dying beast, descending—

Crashing toward Opelucid!

A rampaging, drugged-up Kyurem...

And a falling battleship.

# 530: Gengar: Here's Something Fun for You

"We—we're gonna be crushed!!"

Just as the residents of Opelucid City thought they had survived a doomsday-level ice catastrophe, they now looked up in terror as the massive airship hurtled toward them. Their nerves, barely recovering, tensed once again.

RUMBLE!!

Explosions mixed with falling snow, the darkened sky blotted by the vast falling warship. Its presence pressed down like the weight of the world.

"There's no time to escape…" Despair washed over the citizens.

Shaga stood firm, freshly victorious over Vio, his Haxorus ready at his side.

After the initial shock, Shaga's expression hardened.

As the Gym Leader of Opelucid's Dual Dragon Gym, and as the city's mayor, there were responsibilities he could not run from—he had to face this head-on.

Even if he knew it was impossible to stop that sky-falling behemoth, he had to try.

"All of you!!" Shaga roared. "Evacuate immediately! I'll buy us time!!"

His heavy voice, amplified through the city's broadcast system, echoed far and wide.

Flee?

The people, once paralyzed with fear, jolted at his words.

That's right!

They had Shaga. They had Cynthia, Caitlin, Wanlong, Lenwu—

And most of all—they had Teacher Johan.

The image of Mewtwo blocking Kyurem's Ice Cannon, the way it ripped the cannon apart—engraved in every heart.

"Ditching Kyurem too… Cold-blooded but effective," Johan murmured atop a tall building downtown.

Indeed, Colress didn't hesitate. Had he tried to flee with Kyurem, Johan would've stopped him—but by abandoning both the warship and Kyurem, he erased any chance of pursuit.

Fwoosh—

Mewtwo reappeared beside Johan, its psychic aura pulsing.

But before it could act, Johan stopped it with a hand.

Mewtwo looked puzzled.

This ship, if it crashed… Opelucid wouldn't survive.

Johan rolled up his sleeves and smiled, "Save your strength for the real threat."

The real threat?

Mewtwo's eyes narrowed.

Kyurem.

As for the ship—

"Leave it and the Plasma goons to us," Johan said, turning to Cynthia.

She nodded calmly, unflinching. "Garchomp."

ROAR!!

Her Garchomp howled beside her.

A moment later, a Key Stone in Cynthia's hand glowed brilliantly, enveloping Garchomp in radiant energy.

Mewtwo paused to watch, its gaze softening.

Having felt that bond during its own Mega Evolution, it now truly understood—this was the strength that came from unity between trainer and Pokémon.

It made Mewtwo's inner confusion fade.

Human or Pokémon—what they were didn't matter.

"Mega Evolve!" Cynthia called out.

ROAR!!

Mega Garchomp appeared, sleek and deadly.

Cynthia smiled faintly, cape fluttering, one hand on her hip, the other raised.

"Garchomp, Dragon Rush!"

Mega Garchomp, cloaked in fearsome purple-blue energy, roared and launched itself at the falling warship.

Caitlin was not far behind. Hovering off the ground, her psychic powers surged, her long hair drifting like silk.

Beside her, Gothitelle, Type: Null, and a number of Psychic-types all unleashed their power.

Though weaker than Mewtwo, together they could delay the ship's fall.

Even Mewtwo turned its head in mild surprise.

Meanwhile—

Johan moved.

As a bright white light burst forth, Gengar flashed a toothy grin and gave Mewtwo a "V for victory."

"Gengar, Mega Evolve!!"

Mewtwo watched intently. As light engulfed Johan and Gengar, it sensed something.

Same Mega Evolution. Same trainer.

Yet the bond between Johan and Gengar felt stronger.

If Mega Evolution had added "1" to Mewtwo's power, Gengar's felt like a solid "3".

Even Mewtwo realized—it had much to learn from Gengar.

Shaga, Wanlong, Lenwu—all were watching.

Shaga relaxed slightly.

With Johan, Cynthia, Caitlin united, and himself added in, they just might win time for the city's people to escape.

He looked up at the ship and inhaled deeply. "Altaria!!"

Meanwhile—

Mega Gengar took form, its head covered in spikes, and its third eerie yellow eye blinking open.

"Gengar!"

Johan exhaled. Mega Evolving Gengar was far easier than Mewtwo.

"Ka-kaah!!" Gengar stuck its tongue out, crouching low with glee.

Caitlin and her Pokémon enveloped the ship in psychic fields, slowing its descent.

"I can't hold it long!" she gasped, sweat beading her forehead.

ROAR!!

Mega Garchomp reached the ship's bow and pushed with all its might.

"Dragonite! Ninetales!"

"Draaa!!"

Dragonite appeared beside Garchomp, its body radiating berserker rage.

Outrage—activated!

"Oowoo!!"

Ninetales unleashed the frigid energy it had absorbed earlier.

Permafrost Wall!

A towering ice barrier and shimmering aurora shields formed above the city.

Temperatures plummeted once more.

Shaga saw and bellowed into the broadcast system:

"All Pokémon capable of Light Screen or Reflect—lend your power to Teacher Johan!"

Trainers and residents alike responded, summoning Pokémon to erect barriers.

Still—

This only slowed the ship's descent.

And Gengar?

It had yet to make a big move.

Or rather, it was quietly doing something enormous.

It was... opening a spatial rift.

Confident in its mastery of ghost-type powers, Gengar pulled out its ace—

A smartphone.

Digital Pet—online!

In the Distortion World, Giratina watched a small island float by, peeking into a glass shard at a child's phone screen when it suddenly felt something.

Gengar?

They chatted often.

"Ka-kaah!!" (Lend me your power!!)

Giratina perked up.

Big fight?

It didn't hesitate.

"Garrr!!" (No problem!)

Curious, it asked—

"Ka-kaah?" (How do I get to the Distortion World?)

"Garrr?" (You're coming to play?)

"Ka-kaah." (I'm bringing you something fun.)

"Garrr." (Then—do this.)

Buzz—

A black line split open above Opelucid.

"Kubfu, Tinkaton, Ceruledge, Larvitar, Rotom—assist!"

The little ones lent their strength.

BOOM!!

The Plasma ship erupted in flames—

Only for the fire to freeze, and through the icy inferno emerged a terrifying shape—

Kyurem had awakened!

The chilling aura shook everyone.

"Mewtwo!!"

Johan yelled, prepared for this moment.

They couldn't let Kyurem crash into the city.

"Leave it to me!"

Mewtwo nodded and shot skyward.

ROAR!!

Red-eyed, Kyurem sensed a threat and roared at Mewtwo.

It wanted to destroy everything!

But Mewtwo blinked behind it, grabbed its tail—

SWOOSH—

And dragged Kyurem away from Opelucid.

Kyurem: ???

Meanwhile, the warship—engulfed in explosions—lost control and plummeted.

BOOM BOOM BOOM—

Barriers shattered.

"Still not enough?" Shaga muttered grimly.

Johan: "Gengar!!"

"Ka-kaah!!"

Hovering midair, Mega Gengar flashed an "OK" sign.

Ghost energy burst forth.

From every direction, ghostly forces surged toward Gengar.

Wanlong blinked. "That... that's like Granny Agatha's Gengar…"

"Help Gengar!" she ordered her team.

Suspended midair, Mega Gengar placed one hand above and one below the rift.

"Ka-kaah!!"

It yanked.

The line split open wider—forming a giant pocket!

The falling warship hit the rift's edge—like a dumpling stuck halfway into a bag.

Stuck?

"Dragonite!!"

"Draaa!!"

Dragonite looped and slammed into the back of the warship.

BOOM!!

Inch by inch, the ship went deeper.

Everyone joined in—

"Garchomp!!"

"Gothitelle! Type: Null!"

"Altaria!"

"Golurk! Drifblim!"

BOOM! BOOM! BOOM!

The rift devoured the ship bit by bit.

But the ship was too huge—and still exploding.

"We'll help! Unfezant, Fly!"

"I'm in! Swanna, Tailwind!"

"Pidgeot, Brave Bird!!"

Flying-types from trainers and citizens alike rose into the sky.

One by one, their pushes meant little—

Together—they meant everything.

Just as the final explosions rocked the airship—

WHRR—

It vanished into the rift.

Gengar's Mega form faded. It knelt midair, panting, tongue out.

The rift closed behind it.

Shaga, Cynthia, Caitlin, Wanlong, Lenwu—everyone stood frozen.

The shaken night sky still shimmered with embers.

But now—

ROAR!!!

The city erupted with cheers.

The ship was gone.

The sky, once darkened, now gleamed with moonlight.

Beneath it—

Gengar rose, slipped on sunglasses, placed its hands on its hips—

And flashed a massive grin.

"Ka-kaah!!"

Opelucid City trembled.

"Long live Teacher Johan!!"

"Long live Gengar!!!"

"How can a Gengar be this strong—and this cute?!"

"We're saved!! We're saved!! Waaaah—"

Garchomp, Dragonite, Altaria and others floated beside Gengar.

"Ka-kaah?" (Wasn't I cool just now?)

"Draaa!!" (Super cool!!)

Dragonite was all in.

Even Garchomp gave Gengar a sidelong look—acknowledging it with pride.

Meanwhile, in the Distortion World—

Giratina stared at the Plasma airship now sitting before it, blazing and broken.

It scratched its head.

...Was this the "gift"?

# 531: A Righteous Beatdown?

The moon hung high, the stars sparse.

The residents of Opelucid City and members of the League were still shaken, unable to calm themselves from the shock and joy of what had just transpired.

Nearly encased in ice, the massive airborne warship had exploded like a meteor in the sky. A mysterious and powerful Pokémon, the teamwork of top Trainers, Mega Gengar ripping open a spatial rift, and everyone working together to send that monstrous ship into the void...

Every moment had gripped their hearts.

"Teacher Johan, Miss Cynthia, Miss Caitlin—thank you all so much."

With a long sigh, Shaga offered his heartfelt thanks.

Had Johan not given an early warning, had he not suspected Team Plasma's diversion tactic, there was no way Shaga alone could have defended Opelucid City.

Vio alone was no match, but Vio with the Dark Triad? That would've overwhelmed him.

Not to mention—Team Plasma actually launched their airship and fired the "Kyurem Ice Cannon"—that was completely beyond Shaga's ability to handle.

Of course, Wanlong and Lenwu's arrival had made a significant difference too. Between the two, they'd taken down dozens of Team Plasma members.

Now, with Colress having fled and the airship fallen, most of the Team Plasma operatives had either escaped or been captured.

Cynthia shook her head slightly. "We just took a gamble."

Johan added, "It's not over yet."

Shaga's expression shifted. "You mean… the dragon that was taken away? That was Kyurem?"

"Yes."

Shaga exhaled slowly.

As a Dragon-type specialist and from the family that protected the DNA Splicer, Shaga wasn't unfamiliar with Kyurem.

In fact—

He knew more than most.

Kyurem, dubbed "the strongest dragon on the surface," didn't rely solely on its Ice-type power.

"No wonder…" Shaga muttered. "No wonder the Ice Cannon was so terrifying. If not for your Pokémon, Teacher Johan, I'm afraid the entire city would've been frozen solid."

No wonder Team Plasma had acted so boldly.

While Shaga had been told by Alder that Kyurem may have been captured, he'd been skeptical. Not because he doubted Alder—but because he knew how terrifying Kyurem was. It shouldn't be that easy to catch.

But now, the truth was undeniable.

Given that—

Shaga looked at Johan, memorizing the face of this young Trainer.

When everyone else rushed to Lacunosa to chase Team Plasma, it was this man who uncovered their real target. Whether by intuition or luck didn't matter.

What mattered was—

Even when faced with Team Plasma's full assault, including a legendary Pokémon, he stood firm and stopped them.

And when Plasma, in desperation, tried to sacrifice both ship and dragon to destroy Opelucid, it was Johan who led the effort to avert the catastrophe.

From Shaga's perspective—

Johan was nothing short of a hero.

"Teacher Johan…" Shaga's expression turned solemn. "I believe this item… it's safer in your hands."

Johan's eyes narrowed as he saw what Shaga took out.

The DNA Splicer.

A sacred relic passed down by Shaga's family for generations. Said to be connected to the truth and ideals of the twin dragons. Legend had it only a true hero could be recognized by and use it.

And yet, for generations, no one in Shaga's family had ever been recognized.

"So… Team Plasma was really after the Splicer in your possession," Caitlin murmured.

What they wanted with it remained unclear.

But one thing was certain:

Whoever held the DNA Splicer would become a target of Team Plasma.

This major incident might only be the beginning. Once they regroup, they'd come back.

So the Splicer—precious as it was—was now a blazing-hot potato.

"Teacher Johan," Shaga said awkwardly. "I know holding this might bring you trouble. But I believe… your strength is enough to protect it."

Johan rubbed his brow.

"Trouble" didn't even begin to cover it.

Holding this thing might make Reshiram or Zekrom treat him as an enemy.

As for Team Plasma?

No Genesect, no Kyurem—there wasn't much they could do now. They'd probably fall apart soon.

"Besides…"

Shaga added, "If that was really Kyurem just now, the DNA Splicer might be useful."

Johan's expression shifted slightly.

After all, Kyurem, Zekrom, Reshiram, and the Splicer came from the same origin.

If he could use the Splicer, perhaps it could allow communication with Kyurem?

He looked to Cynthia and Caitlin.

Cynthia nodded gently. "If Shaga says so, you should take it."

She had already done research into the Splicer and its lore. If it stayed in Shaga's hands, who's to say Opelucid wouldn't face another attack?

With Johan, she trusted it would be safe.

And if not—she was there to help.

Besides…

"The DNA Splicer is quite helpful for Dragon- and Ice-type Pokémon."

Really?

In Black/White, the Splicer wasn't present—it was the Light/Dark Stones.

But in Black 2/White 2, the Splicer replaced those stones as the core item.

Dragons and Ice-types… This would be good for Dragonite and Ninetales.

Given all that—

Johan accepted it. "Very well… I'll borrow the DNA Splicer and see if it can help with Kyurem."

"Excellent!!"

Shaga accepted Johan's "borrow" phrasing with a smile.

Didn't matter. As long as Johan had it, that was enough.

If he ever gave it back… well, if Shaga was still alive by then, they'd talk.

As for Johan—

Once this World Championship was over, he was heading back to Hoenn.

Let Kyurem or the dragons come find him there if they dared.

"So I'll leave things here to you, Shaga. I need to check on Kyurem," Johan said gravely.

"We're coming too," Cynthia and Caitlin said at once.

Kyurem's power was immense. Even if Mewtwo had carried it off, it hadn't gone far.

Even now, snow still fell over Opelucid.

Shaga nodded heavily. "Please… take care of it."

He had his hands full.

Plasma remnants remained. While the chaos had mostly subsided, order hadn't fully returned.

As mayor, he had to restore peace.

Besides, he doubted he'd be of much use anyway.

Most of his Pokémon were spent after fighting Vio and the Dark Triad.

No need to drag the team down.

"Let's go!"

Johan and Cynthia locked eyes, then turned to Caitlin.

She pouted, then placed a hand on both their shoulders.

Buzz—

Teleport!

Using Mewtwo's psychic presence as a beacon, it was uncomfortable—but fast.

"Eh?"

Wanlong and Lenwu arrived a beat too late.

"They're gone…"

Shaga explained everything quickly, and they decided to help stabilize Opelucid and finish rounding up Plasma while waiting for the others to return.

Meanwhile—

In a forest outside the city.

"Calm down!"

Mewtwo was encased in a spherical psychic shield, gritting its teeth as it blocked Kyurem's relentless icy blasts.

The area had become a frozen wasteland.

Snow blanketed everything. Cold surged outward, burying the forest in a blizzard.

ROAR!!

Kyurem, eyes blood-red, raged uncontrollably.

When Johan and the others appeared, it felt like they'd returned to Mt. Coronet's peak.

Maybe worse.

Johan's head throbbed.

He hadn't expected Colress to pump Kyurem so full of "stimulants" before setting it loose.

If only Kyurem had been sealed inside the ship before it woke up. Then it would've gone straight to the Distortion World and been someone else's problem.

Giratina: ???

"Let's see…"

Johan pulled out the DNA Splicer and injected it with Aura.

"Let's see if this works…"

"Johan! We're here!!"

Hoofbeats rang out.

Keldeo and the Swords of Justice had arrived!

They'd been recovering since the Genesect incident. Now, fully healed, they were ready.

Perfect timing!

Time for a righteous beatdown!

"Yoji!!" Larvitar's eyes lit up.

My red steed has arrived!

Buzz—

The DNA Splicer pulsed in Johan's hands.

Huh?

Kyurem paused.

Far away, atop a lonely tower, a milky-white orb trembled faintly.

# 532: Mewtwo Finds Its Purpose

"ROAR!!"

Kyurem roared furiously, its icy aura growing ever more intense.

"Johan, what should we do?"

Staring at the berserk Kyurem, Keldeo, though not as terrified as before, still gulped nervously and asked.

What should they do? What else—

Subdue it.

"Go!" Johan gave a light wave of his hand.

At his word, Ninetales, Dragonite, Tinkaton, and the rest surged forward at once.

Cynthia and Caitlin's Pokémon followed immediately after. Then the three Swords of Justice leapt in as well.

"Uh?"

Keldeo blinked twice at the sight.

Was it really okay for this many of them to gang up on one Kyurem?

Wasn't this just a straight-up beatdown?

But Johan told him—

"This is a righteous beatdown."

The longer Kyurem rampaged, the more the environment suffered. Nearby wild Pokémon would also be harmed.

That's why they had to strike—no matter what.

This was the same reason the other Swords of Justice hadn't hesitated for a second.

Keldeo was just a bit slow to realize it.

But after hearing Johan's brief explanation, Keldeo's expression hardened.

For justice's beatdown—no, for the environment and the Pokémon!

He galloped forward.

…Though for some reason, he felt a tiny bit happy doing so?

Must be a delusion.

At the same time, Johan took out the DNA Splicer and channeled Aura into it once more.

The Splicer's connection to Kyurem ran deeper than the Rainbow Wing to Ho-Oh or the Silver Wing to Lugia.

Last time he'd channeled Aura into it, he had felt Kyurem's response. If things went right, maybe this battle didn't need to happen at all.

And sure enough—

Johan's perception was pulled into a frozen world.

Every inch was covered in ice crystals. All he could see was frost, nothing more.

But some places were different.

Right before him stood a massive ice sculpture of Kyurem. On either side of it were hollow altar-like slots—presumably meant for Zekrom and Reshiram.

Understanding clicked in Johan's mind.

Then—

He raised his hand and slowly pulled away wisps of dark red energy from Kyurem's sculpture.

This was… Defusion?

Huh. He could do that?

As the violent energy that had empowered yet unbalanced Kyurem was stripped away, the Pokémon's rampage began to settle.

Mewtwo and the others felt the change—the resistance from Kyurem was growing weaker.

Finally, when Johan peeled away the last of the red energy, Kyurem shut its eyes and stopped moving.

[Thank you, human.]

A deep, serious voice echoed in Johan's mind.

"Kyurem?"

[Yes, it is I.]

A pause.

[Thank you for your help.]

Polite for a dragon-type legendary, Johan thought.

Dialga, Palkia, Rayquaza… all of them were arrogant and hot-headed beyond belief.

Kyurem's composure was… weirdly refreshing.

But maybe this was why it had agreed to test the Swords of Justice before—and why it had frozen them to protect them from Team Plasma.

Kyurem was a godlike Pokémon that understood right from wrong.

"I'm glad you're better now." Johan sighed in relief.

Kyurem's ice sculpture grew more lifelike—it was "looking" at Johan.

And then—

[One more thing… could you ask them to let me go?]

Huh?

Johan opened his eyes—

Mewtwo, the Swords of Justice, Keldeo, Garchomp, Dragonite, Tinkaton… had Kyurem completely pinned to the snowy ground.

"Yoji!!"

Larvitar sprinted like a ninja over to Kyurem's massive head. It raised one green foot—

One good kick for justice!

But—

STARE

Kyurem opened its eyes and glared at Larvitar.

"Yoji?!"

Larvitar froze, foot suspended in midair. Then it slowly retracted the kick, clasped its hands behind its back, tilted its head to the sky at a 45-degree angle, and started whistling.

What a lovely moon tonight~

Kyurem: "…"

Johan pressed his lips together. Was it possible…

That Kyurem was only acting polite because it had no choice?

"…Alright guys, it's calm now."

Kyurem left with the Swords of Justice.

After being captured, used as a weapon, and nearly exploded—all for nothing—it really just wanted to rest.

Though Kyurem was angry at Colress and Team Plasma, it needed time to recover.

To stop it from rampaging again, Johan promised: if given the chance, he'd help Kyurem settle the score.

As for if Team Plasma captured Kyurem again?

Well, with the losses they'd suffered—no Genesect, no airship, no Kyurem, and half their force wiped out—if they could still catch Kyurem, Kyurem might as well freeze itself with a block of tofu and smash its head in.

Plus—

The Swords of Justice and Keldeo would be keeping watch around Giant Chasm from now on.

With all of them together, Team Plasma wouldn't even think about making another move unless they had Zekrom or Reshiram.

With Kyurem's departure, the incident was finally over.

Johan could breathe.

He had just wanted to help some Pokémon get home—how had it turned into this mess?

Back in Opelucid City.

Under Shaga's direction, most Team Plasma members were arrested. League staff were clearing lingering frost.

Although Mewtwo had intercepted most of the Ice Cannon's blast, plenty of residual energy had affected the city.

Patches of unmelting ice remained throughout.

Some couldn't even be removed with fire.

But that wasn't Johan's problem anymore.

At Shaga's arrangement, Johan and his team were housed in a luxury villa.

Honestly—

He was exhausted.

One sleepless night fighting Team Plasma and Kyurem. The moment Johan sat down on the bed, his eyes were already closing.

His body could take it, but his mind?

After Mega Evolving both Mewtwo and Gengar, and trying to control the DNA Splicer—he was fried.

Still, there was one last thing to do before sleeping.

"Oowoo~"

Ninetales curled beside him, while Gengar floated overhead clutching a ghostly blanket, already asleep.

It had opened the rift to the Distortion World and shoved in an exploding airship—no wonder it was wiped out.

"This is from Kyurem."

Johan took out the DNA Splicer again. Now, the Kyurem sculpture was gone. Only three empty slots remained—just like before, when Zekrom and Reshiram hadn't been placed.

But Kyurem had left something behind for Ninetales—or rather, for Johan. A thank-you gift.

"Oowoo?"

As Ninetales touched the Splicer, it froze slightly.

A move tutor?

On the Splicer, Ninetales saw a frozen screen showing how Kyurem had used Glaciate.

"Study it. If you can't learn the move, at least study the frost-condensing technique."

"Oowoo~"

Ninetales nodded seriously.

It had seen true Ice-type power and realized its own shortcomings.

Meanwhile, on the balcony—

Mewtwo stood in silence, watching the rising sun.

Not far away, a man in a suit, a Herdier, and a little girl were playing in the snow.

The girl giggled like bells, chasing Herdier in circles, building snowmen.

"That's Trainer Daddy!" she said, pointing to a vague lump of snow.

The man looked awkward.

"This is Herdier!" she pointed to a smaller snowman.

"Bark!" Herdier wagged happily.

"Daddy and Herdier are heroes!" the girl said with awe.

The man puffed his chest. "Of course! Daddy and Herdier fought too!"

They had helped. Kind of.

"I'll be a Trainer when I grow up—just like Teacher Johan!"

"…Not Daddy?" the man blinked.

"Hehehe~" the girl laughed. "But you're just as amazing as Teacher Johan!"

"Right, right…"

To a child, their parent was always the greatest hero.

He began bragging about his younger years.

His wife gently joined them, smiling as she listened. Herdier trotted in circles.

The woman exchanged a look with her husband, full of silent gratitude—

Thank you, Teacher Johan, the hero who saved our city.

"You saved them," Mewtwo's voice rang in Johan's mind.

"No." Johan smiled, gripping the balcony railing. "We saved them, Mewtwo."

Mewtwo pressed its lips together.

Together?

Watching the girl laugh, seeing Herdier bounce about—

Mewtwo thought of Mewtwo-2, Charmander-2, Squirtle-2, Bulbasaur-2…

It hadn't saved them.

But maybe now… maybe they would be proud of what it had become?

Its face didn't change—but a warmth stirred inside. A strange, comforting feeling.

"I think… I've found a little bit of my purpose."

It remembered Johan once told it—the meaning of existence is something to be found, not something defined.

Back then, Mewtwo hadn't fully understood.

Now… it did.

Johan smiled at the rising sun. "Isn't that wonderful?"

If Mewtwo could find its purpose, it wouldn't need to ask "Who am I?" or "Why am I here?" anymore.

"Yeah." Mewtwo nodded. For once, its heart felt light.

Human or Pokémon—it had found its path.

"Now that this is over, I think I'll head back for a bit."

Because of Johan's influence, Mewtwo's view of Team Rocket had softened slightly.

It had come here to help, but it still had things to do.

Like… finding Mewtwo-2 and the others.

Porygon-Z could help.

And after this Mega Evolution, Mewtwo had new insights into its powers.

It needed time to reflect.

"Alright."

Johan didn't object. Mewtwo was free.

"Also," Johan added, "if you ever want to help more Pokémon—or if you ever need help—you can come to me."

In the original timeline, Mewtwo founded an island refuge for cloned and wild Pokémon.

After the Genesect incident, Johan had similar thoughts.

"Okay."

Mewtwo nodded, then soared into the sky.

Two days had passed since Johan saved Opelucid.

The media had gone wild, and Johan's fame in Unova skyrocketed.

His power rivaled even Champion Alder.

A hero who turned the tide—that's the kind of story Unovans love.

Now, with their eyes opened, the League intensified its crackdown on Team Plasma.

And so—

In N's Castle, Unova.

In the round-table chamber sat Ghetsis, face grim, surrounded by six Sages and, in the corner, Colress—legs crossed, looking unconcerned.

This entire disaster had been to support Colress's research.

The cost was immense—and it failed.

But Colress, the operation's commander, looked utterly unbothered.

Everyone was furious.

But what good was that?

The League's crackdown was so intense, Team Plasma couldn't even leave their hideouts.

They needed a scapegoat.

Ghetsis was glad he'd prepared one.

Just then—

BOOM—

Dark clouds swirled above N's Castle. Thunder cracked.

# 533: N’s Awakening

"So this is Team Plasma…"

Staring at the screen of his tablet, N was shaken to the core as he watched the massive Plasma Frigate descend and unleash a devastating freeze cannon, attempting to engulf all of Opelucid City in ice.

He also saw footage of the attack on the Nacrene Museum by Team Plasma, along with waves of online condemnation aimed at them.

In an instant, N's belief in Team Plasma's noble ideals shattered as he saw them become the target of public outcry.

Their actions now stood in stark contrast to their stated mission—completely opposed, even.

Scene after scene of reality played before him, and with each one, N's worldview crumbled further.

"N…"

His half-sisters, Barbera and Helena, watched him with deep concern.

Unlike N, they had endured far less of Ghetsis's indoctrination. Their understanding of Team Plasma and their foster father's true motives was far clearer.

To prevent N from falling further into deception, the sisters had taken advantage of the chaos within Team Plasma to sneak into his confinement and show him the truth.

There was no doubt.

This truth left an immense impact on N.

He had once hoped to persuade Ghetsis to use Team Plasma's resources to build a haven where Pokémon could live freely—a sanctuary for those who had suffered.

But now…

It turned out that Team Plasma was the very cause of those tragedies, all while parading around with a "save the Pokémon" façade!

N slowly lowered his head, his teal eyes hidden beneath the brim of his hat, and gripped the tablet tightly.

The footage continued to play.

Johan, alongside his Pokémon and Mewtwo, boldly resisting the Plasma Frigate, came into view.

In that moment—

A glimmer of light flashed in N's eyes, like the last hope in his heart.

Even as Team Plasma, the organization he had spent over a decade with, engaged in the very horrors he wished to stop, Johan still stood unwavering—using his strength to help Pokémon and others.

At least someone still believed in him!

Taking a deep breath, N relaxed slightly and forced a smile for his worried sisters.

"I'm fine."

Barbera and Helena exchanged glances. Barbera bit her lip and whispered, "N, you need to leave this place."

Huh?

N was stunned for a moment, then quickly understood.

He wasn't stupid—quite the opposite. He was far more perceptive than most.

Thanks to their warning, he immediately realized that with Team Plasma in deep trouble, the best way to shift blame was to hand over their "King."

If he got arrested, Team Plasma could retreat underground again.

He hadn't thought of this before, blinded by the belief that Team Plasma was a force for good.

Or perhaps… he had tried to force himself to believe it.

But now that illusion had shattered, and the truth became crystal clear.

N wasn't afraid of being captured.

He had grown up locked away in a dark, tiny room, with only broken toys and abused Pokémon as company.

But he couldn't afford to be captured.

He still had dreams to fulfill. He still had to help more Pokémon. He couldn't be made into Team Plasma's scapegoat.

"What about you two?" he asked.

Helena quickly interjected, "Don't worry about us. We have our own way out."

That made sense.

He was the real key to Team Plasma.

With that in mind, N didn't hesitate any longer. He looked northwest.

"All this time, I've felt like a voice from that direction was calling me. This time, I'm going to find out what it is."

And after that—he'd find Johan.

With that thought, N hurriedly bid farewell to his sisters and ran out of the castle.

Most of the Plasma members didn't dare stop him. Those who did, couldn't overcome the strength of N's Pokémon.

Meanwhile—

After N departed, Barbera and Helena began creating a ruckus within the castle to draw attention away from him.

They could have escaped, but that would've risked N being hunted immediately. Instead, they stayed behind to cause chaos and buy him time.

This was something N hadn't anticipated.

"N, run fast. You have to get away from here."

"N, don't ever come back."

Barbera and Helena clasped hands, placing their hopes in the brother they loved.

Opelucid City.

Living in the same villa with Johan and the others, Wanlong once again approached him, clutching a freshly completed novel.

"Johan, could you give me some feedback?"

Johan looked helplessly toward Cynthia, only to receive an encouraging smile as she continued playing with a young Larvitar.

Good grief…

Johan had originally been intrigued by Wanlong's novels.

In a Pokémon world with so little entertainment, having something to read was a blessing.

Plus, Wanlong was a famous novelist.

And not just that—she was known for her stunning figure, a far cry from the flat-chested Caitlin.

But still—

He never understood why Wanlong was both a novelist and a Ghost-type trainer… until he read her books.

They were filled with bizarre, eerie tales and legends—exactly the kind of stuff that gave you nightmares if read at night.

Johan strongly suspected the reason he dreamt of being attacked by Mega Mewtwo X was because of her book.

With no way out, he reluctantly accepted the novel, all while Wanlong gazed at him expectantly.

Her stories weren't that scary.

But in a world like theirs, imagination was a dangerous thing.

Once you let it run wild, even your friends could start to look suspicious.

Thankfully, salvation arrived.

Champion of Unova—Alder!

When Alder learned about the crisis in Opelucid City, he had immediately planned to rush over.

But the crisis ended almost as quickly as it began. By the time Alder left Nacrene, Shaga had already informed him it was over.

Still, Alder understood just how dangerous the situation had been.

It wasn't an exaggeration to say that without Johan's foresight and everyone's efforts, Opelucid City would've suffered massive damage.

So, once things were settled in Nacrene, Alder made his way to Opelucid.

"Mr. Alder, come, come, sit!"

Johan eagerly led him to a table in the yard.

Meanwhile, Gengar had just finished playing with Dragonite and crept over.

"Ha ha ha—" Alder's laugh was as hearty as ever. "No need to be so polite, Johan."

His enthusiasm caught even Alder a bit off-guard.

"Of course, of course…"

Johan glanced at Wanlong, now deep in thought again, and sighed in relief.

Then he saw Alder—

Alder: "zzZ"

Urshifu: "zzZ"

Honestly, if you didn't know better, you'd think they were related.

"Oh, right." Alder suddenly snapped awake and sat up. "Johan, I heard the International Police are setting up a special task force for Team Plasma. The leader's spot is open. Interested?"

A task force under the International Police?

These were usually temporary units, but with enormous power—like the UB task force in Alola, headed by Battle Tower boss Anabel, who had access to nearly the entire Alola League's resources.

So this new unit? Definitely no lightweight.

Still…

Let Alder deal with Team Plasma.

"Not interested," Johan replied instantly.

"All right."

Alder didn't press.

After all, it wasn't like he was offering him the position of Unova's Champion or Elite Four.

"One more thing."

"Hm?"

"Drayden from Icirrus City says there's been strange activity at Dragonspiral Tower."

Dragonspiral Tower?

Johan blinked.

That was where Zekrom was said to be sealed.

Strange activity?

But hadn't Zekrom already appeared—back when Johan arrived in Lacunosa Town?

Unless… Reshiram is the one sealed in that tower?

Either way—

Not his business.

He just wanted a peaceful tournament experience in Unova, and the opening was just days away!

"Oh! Wanlong, I just thought of a new idea!"

He bolted to his feet.

"Really?" Wanlong's eyes sparkled behind her glasses.

As Johan dashed off, Alder looked bewildered.

???

Where'd that enthusiasm go?

Alder scratched his head, still mid-thought. "All I was going to say is… Dragonspiral Tower has a long history. It's full of Ghost-types. Might be useful for your Gengar."

"Ka-kaah!"

Gengar, hiding nearby, perked up at the mention of a reward and emerged from the shadows.

"Gengaaa~~"

Seeing Gengar, Alder chuckled warmly.

"Ka-kaah~~"

Gengar waved back with a grin.

After a brief stare-off—

"Oh, right." Alder pulled out a fist-sized, jagged black stone and handed it to Gengar. "Same deal for you and Johan. Help him keep it safe."

"Ka-kaah?"

Gengar accepted the stone, puzzled.

What was this?

He even considered licking it.

"I don't know either. But apparently Team Plasma wasn't just after meteorites at the Nacrene Museum. This was something else they wanted."

Alder stretched and added, "Maybe Johan can figure it out. I'll leave it with you two."

Hmm…

Gengar nodded.

# 534: Meloetta and Undella Town!

April 27, Sunday, sunny skies.

"Johan, please battle me!"

Marshal took up a martial artist's stance in the garden, solemnly issuing a challenge to Johan.

As Alder's student and a dedicated fighter, Marshal had actually wanted to battle Johan ever since the Opelucid City incident.

But the timing hadn't been right back then, and later there was just too much going on, so it was postponed until now.

Marshal deeply respected Johan's strength—especially the moment he and Mewtwo had stood firm against Kyurem's ice cannon.

Still, to shrink back just because of a strength gap—that's not what a true Trainer would do.

A Trainer must always challenge stronger opponents, grow through battles, and become better.

Victory or defeat is important, sure—but the battle itself matters more.

"Marshal, I think… maybe we could save this match for the 'Junior Masters World Championship.' What do you say?"

Johan had already anticipated this request, and prepared an answer.

With the tournament only days away, it made little sense to battle now when they might end up facing off during the real event. Fighting now could affect either's momentum going into the championship.

Marshal thought for a moment and nodded. Johan had a point.

And as for whether they'd meet at the tournament…

Marshal was confident—in his own abilities and in Johan's. As long as they kept winning, they'd meet.

Having made his decision, Marshal didn't linger in Opelucid City. Instead, he set off on foot for Mistralton City, framing it as one final training journey with his Pokémon.

This had been a small episode during Johan's time in Opelucid City.

At the moment, Johan was preparing—more or less—for the Junior Masters World Championship.

Not just him.

Cynthia and Caitlin were too.

And from Hoenn came Steven, from Sinnoh came Flint, Volkner, and Aaron, from Kanto came Lance, Lorelei, and others—all arriving in the Unova region.

They weren't all headed to Opelucid, though; each had their own agenda. But the meeting point was set for Mistralton City.

After another large-scale crackdown by the Unova League against Team Plasma, and determining that they wouldn't resurface anytime soon, preparations for the championship officially began.

The hype around this championship was immense in Unova.

Especially after it was revealed Johan would participate.

As the hero who saved Opelucid City, Johan's stand against Team Plasma had protected countless residents and Pokémon, winning him massive support overnight.

And not just in Unova—across the entire Pokémon world, excitement was high.

Lance from Kanto, Cynthia from Sinnoh, and others had massive followings too.

Having said goodbye to Shaga, Johan, Cynthia, and Caitlin headed to Undella Town.

A seaside village where Cynthia and Caitlin often vacationed during breaks—a perfect spot for their "final training."

And the method of travel?

Flying and train were both options, but Johan and the others chose… to drive.

Partly because the road from Opelucid City to Undella Town was smooth, and partly to enjoy the scenery.

To be honest, ever since arriving in Unova, Johan had been rushing or busy—he'd barely had a chance to appreciate the region's beauty.

When Shaga learned of this, he generously provided them with an off-road jeep.

Thus, with Rotom driving, Johan, Cynthia, and Caitlin hit the road toward the coastal town.

"Rotom, don't go too fast—we're here to enjoy the scenery," Johan reminded.

"Understood, Roto!"

Rotom's voice buzzed from the engine.

"A psychologist afraid of speed and heights, tsk tsk…"

Caitlin teased Johan from the back seat, while Cynthia simply smiled.

Those close to Johan knew about his "fears."

In the passenger seat, Johan clicked his tongue, propping his chin with one hand as he gazed at the passing landscape, muttering:

"Mewtwo's been gone a while… next time, it can come find me in Undella."

Hearing "Mewtwo," Caitlin's face instantly stiffened.

The fearless Caitlin… afraid of Mewtwo? Johan had her weakness now.

He couldn't resist pressing it!

That's just the kind of petty person he was.

Gengar sat in the driver's seat, grinning and holding the steering wheel.

Since Rotom was actually driving, Gengar was just pretending—and enjoying it thoroughly.

"Ka-kaah~~"

What a nice day~ Grandpa's feelin' good~~~

In high spirits, Gengar even started humming a tune.

It sounded… well… not great. But at least it wasn't causing a car crash.

"By the way…"

Johan looked at Gengar. "Did we forget something?"

"Ka-kaah?"

Gengar scratched its head.

Huh? Did we?

In the Ruins of the World, Giratina stared at the wreckage of the Plasma Frigate.

What kind of "gift" was this?

"Ka-kaah~~"

Gengar didn't care. It just wanted to keep singing.

"Ka-kaah~~ Ka-kaah~~ Ka~kaah~~"

Johan quietly pulled out a pair of earplugs.

Cynthia and Caitlin, however, didn't seem bothered by Gengar's howling and were chatting happily in the back.

The jeep drove smoothly along the road, accompanied by Gengar's "melody."

"Melo~~"

In the forest between Lacunosa Town and Undella, a gentle tune floated like a spring breeze, drawing wild Pokémon to pause and listen.

A Pokémon, cloaked in invisibility, danced lightly through the trees.

Sometimes visible, sometimes not.

Flowing green hair, a gem-studded headband, and a black mic-like accessory on its head.

"Melo?"

Reaching a cliff edge, the little Pokémon was thrilled—everyone always loved its song.

Then it heard a strange "Ka-kaah~ Ka-kaah~" coming from the road below.

Not pleasant… but cheerful.

No technique, but full of feeling.

"Melo~~"

Thrilled to find a fellow "singer," it revealed itself and peeked down from the cliff.

"Gaa?"

Just then—

A Mandibuzz flying overhead spotted the "green caterpillar" hair from afar. Its eyes lit up.

It tucked its wings, swooped down like lightning.

"Melo!!"

Caught off guard, the little one panicked—it wasn't a fighter.

Mandibuzz realized it wasn't a caterpillar after all, but still, annoyed at being "fooled," it pecked.

"Melo!!"

Hurt and off-balance, Meloetta slipped and fell from the cliff.

Mandibuzz didn't stop—it dived after it.

Meanwhile, on the jeep, Gengar noticed the commotion and licked Johan.

Johan removed an earplug, bit into a Cheri Berry, and asked grumpily:

"What is it?"

"Ka-kaah!"

Gengar pointed—Mandibuzz was attacking something, and it had fallen.

Johan's face changed.

"Gengar!"

"Ka-kaah!"

Gengar ripped open a dimensional rift and vanished.

Next second—it appeared midair beside Mandibuzz, cackling, lightning in hand.

BAM!

Mandibuzz got smacked with a Thunder Punch and tumbled away, dazed, fleeing in terror.

The jeep stopped under the cliff.

A small, injured Pokémon staggered from the bushes and collapsed on the road.

"Melo…"

Cynthia and Caitlin jumped out with Johan.

"Meloetta?" Cynthia was surprised.

The mythical Pokémon from Unova—Meloetta!

Johan's mouth twitched.

Oh great. Can't even drive without running into a legendary.

"It's hurt," Caitlin said.

"Ka-kaah?"

Gengar floated back.

Despite his grumbling, Johan scooped up the faint Meloetta.

"Melo… Melo…"

Its light green eyes fluttered, then shut in pain.

Its body was light, its cheeks red, its temperature clearly off.

Gengar laid a soft mat down.

Johan worked quickly, using a healing spray on its wounds.

"It has a fever too?" Cynthia asked.

"Yeah." Johan nodded.

He remembered the original BW plot.

Meloetta had been attacked by a trio and found by Ash and friends afterward—fevered then, too.

"Probably from fear."

Like a child—scared enough to spike a fever.

Despite being mythical, Meloetta was incredibly frail—even compared to Shaymin.

A true "glass cannon."

"Yoji~~"

Larvitar toddled over with a wet towel, blushed at the sight of Meloetta.

"Yoji… (〃￣ω￣〃）"

So cute~

Johan placed his hand on her wound, channeling aura to soothe it, while tossing out a Poké Ball.

"Ninetales, give us some crushed ice."

"Oowoo~~"

Ninetales exhaled a breath of cold, forming ice shards. Cynthia wrapped them in cloth and set it on Meloetta's forehead.

Meloetta visibly relaxed, even humming softly at Johan's gentle aura massage.

"Gengar, brew some herbal root tea," Johan ordered.

"Ka-kaah~~"

Gengar got to work—table, teapots, herbs.

Caitlin blinked. "You carry a whole tea set with you?"

"Ka-kaah!!" Gengar puffed up proudly.

"Don't look so smug," Johan muttered. "Hurry up."

"Ka-kaah~~"

Rotom zapped the kettle to a boil, and Gengar brewed the tea.

This bitter stuff would lower friendship in the games—but Gengar drank it daily, and Johan never noticed any drop in affection.

"Meloetta, drink this." Johan gently lifted her head, blew on the tea, and offered it.

"Melo…"

Flushed and weak, she opened her mouth reflexively.

The bitter tea hit her tongue—she cried out and spit it back up.

"Melo…"

So bitter…

Johan sighed. "It's not bad, really. It'll help you feel better."

He demonstrated—gulped it down himself.

Hiss—his tongue went numb.

Damn, this stuff was nasty!

Still, he faked a straight face.

"Melo…"

Seeing him do it, she finally drank.

Still bitter—but at least she wasn't alone.

The fever began to subside, and everyone sighed in relief.

"You're surprisingly good at this," Caitlin said.

"School taught me well."

He had studied it.

"But this little one…"

Johan looked at the sleeping Meloetta with a frown.

It didn't have much battle ability. Its real purpose was as the key to the "Abyssal Ruins."

Where the Reveal Glass was sealed—used to show the true forms of the Forces of Nature: Landorus, Tornadus, and Thundurus.

Among them, the legendary "Bug God" Landorus in its Incarnate Form.

In the original story, Giovanni himself came for Meloetta to unleash the trio.

But now?

Giovanni probably didn't have such ambitions. That plan may never have existed.

No need to worry about Meloetta causing trouble.

"Let's take her. No way she can stay in the wild like this," Cynthia suggested.

"Yeah."

Even if trouble came, Johan couldn't just leave her alone.

Her "guardians" would probably come looking soon.

Carrying her, they resumed their journey to Undella Town.

Undella Town.

Located in eastern Unova—a beautiful seaside village backed by Reversal Mountain and facing the sea. A perfect retirement spot.

Once home to the wealthy Bourgeois family, now empty save for a grand villa.

Caitlin owned a large beachfront house there. Step out the back door, and there's golden sand and endless ocean.

Sunlight, sea breeze, glimmering waves.

The comfort made Johan lazy—who could think about "training" now?

Not that he had planned much training anyway.

Just regular routines—Gengar's boxing, Ninetales' snow sculpting, Dragonite's flying.

What Johan really wanted was quiet time to "digest" everything they'd experienced in Unova.

Like Ninetales' ice control, Gengar learning from Mewtwo's "psy-strike," Ceruledge's swordplay…

As long as everything went smoothly, Johan felt he could easily reach the top 8 in the championship—maybe even top 16 at worst.

If the matchups were fair, maybe even the finals.

If not… well, then no one gets to be fair.
